# Assumptions register

Document number: AQS-REG-002
Revision: A
Date: 2026-10-15
Authority: AQS-PLAN-007 task X-GOV-07. Generated by `docs-authority/_assumptions_register.py`; do not edit by hand. Curated entries live in `docs-authority/assumptions_manual.json`.

| Rev | Description | Date |
| --- | --- | --- |
| A | First issue. | 2026-10-15 |

Rule. Every new assumption gets a curated entry in `assumptions_manual.json` in the same commit that introduces it, with value, units, where used, basis, sensitivity, the measurement or test that replaces it, and the owning plan task. The scanned sections list every study or assumed value already in the repo. They have no individual sensitivity assessment; the owning task assesses it when it closes.

Counts: 269 curated, 550 graph, 22 model params, 103 document lines. Total 944. Scanned entries completed with basis and replacing test: 24 (from `docs-authority/assumptions_complete.json`, keyed by the Where column).
